<div class="modal fade" id="regionModal" role="dialog" tabindex="-1">
<div class="modal-dialog" role="document">
<div class="modal-content">
<div class="modal-header">
<button aria-label="Close" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" type="button"><span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>
<h4 class="modal-title">Выберите регион</h4>
</div>
<div class="modal-body">
</div>
<div class="modal-footer">
<button class="btn btn-default" data-dismiss="modal" type="button">Закрыть</button>
</div>
</div><!-- /.modal-content -->
</div><!-- /.modal-dialog -->
</div><!-- /.modal -->
<script>

	window.onload = function() {
		$("body").on("click",".changeRegion", function(){
			$('#regionModal').modal('toggle');
				$('#regionModal .modal-body').html('Загрузка регионов...');

				$.ajax({
					url: "/city/getRegions",
					type: 'get',
					cache: true,
					dataType: 'json',
					success: function (serverAnswer, status, xhr) {
						if (xhr.status === 200) {
							//console.log(serverAnswer);
							var s = '';
							var htmlData = '';//'<ul class="list-unstyled">';
							$.each( serverAnswer, function( key, value ) {
								var s1 = value.name.charAt(0);
								if(s != s1){
									if(htmlData != ''){
										htmlData += "</ul></div>";
									}
									s = s1;
									htmlData += "<div class='col-md-6'><h2>"+s+"</h2><ul class='list-unstyled'>";
									htmlData += '<li><a href="javascript:void(0)" class="selectRegion" data-id="'+value.id+'">'+value.name+'</a></li>';
								}
								else
								{
									htmlData += '<li><a href="javascript:void(0)" class="selectRegion" data-id="'+value.id+'">'+value.name+'</a></li>';
								}
								//htmlData += '<li><a href="javascript:void(0)" class="selectRegion" data-id="'+value.id+'">'+value.name+'</a></li>';
							});

							//htmlData += '</ul>';

							$('#regionModal .modal-body').html("<div class='row'>"+htmlData+"</div>");
							
						}
					},
					error: function (xhr) {}
				});

		});

		$("body").on("click",".selectRegion", function(){

			var _this = $(this);
			$(".changeRegion").text(_this.text());
			$('#regionModal .modal-body').html('Загрузка городов...');

			$.ajax({
				url: "/city/getCities/" + _this.data("id"),
				type: 'get',
				cache: true,
				dataType: 'json',
				success: function (serverAnswer, status, xhr) {

					if (xhr.status === 200) {
						//console.log(serverAnswer);
						var htmlData = '<option value="0" >-- Город</option>';
						$.each( serverAnswer, function( key, value ) {
							htmlData += '<option value="'+value.id+'" >'+value.name+'</option>';
						});

						//htmlData += '</ul>';

						$('.changeRegionSelect').html(htmlData);
						$('#regionModal').modal('toggle');
					}
				},
				error: function (xhr) {}
			});
		});
	};

	var myForm = document.getElementById('form-jobs');
	myForm.onsubmit = function() {
		$("input,select",$('#form-jobs')).each(function() {
			if($(this).val().trim() == "" || $(this).val().trim() == "0") {
				$(this).attr("name",''); 
			}
		});

		var ar = [];
		if($('#city').val() != 0)
		{
			ar.push('city='+$('#city').val());
		}

		if($('#schedule').val() != 0)
		{
			ar.push('schedule='+$('#schedule').val());
		}

		if($('#experience').val() != 0)
		{
			ar.push('experience='+$('#experience').val());
		}

		if($('#priceMin').val() != 0)
		{
			ar.push('priceMin='+$('#priceMin').val());
		}

		if($('#priceMax').val() != 0)
		{
			ar.push('priceMax='+$('#priceMax').val());
		}

		var str = '';
		if (ar.length > 0) {
			str = '?' + ar.join('&');
		}

		window.location.replace('/jobs/'+ $( "#category option:selected" ).data('url') + str);
		window.location.href = '/jobs/'+ $( "#category option:selected" ).data('url') + str;

		return false;
		//myForm.action='/jobs/'+ $( "#category option:selected" ).data('url');
	};
</script>

<div class="panel-heading text-center">
<h1 class="panel-title"><strong class="text-info">Вакансии в разных городах</strong></h1>
</div>
<div class="panel-body">
<ul class="list-inline">
<li class="lead" style="font-size: 16px;"><a href="/jobs/all?city=1264">
